 253:     private int liquidityConfidence(LiquidityContextStatus status, int observations) {
 254:         int base = switch (status) {
 255:             case BULLISH_SUPPORT -> 95;
 256:             case BALANCED, DISABLED -> 85;
 257:             case LEARNING -> 55;
 258:             case INSUFFICIENT_DATA_HOLD -> 25;
 259:             case UNAVAILABLE -> 40;
 260:             case BEARISH_PRESSURE, STOP_EXPOSED -> 35;
 261:             case TARGET_BLOCKED, THIN_LIQUIDITY -> 15;
 262:             case WALL_WEAKENING -> 65;
 263:         };
 264:         if (observations <= 0 && status != LiquidityContextStatus.DISABLED) {
 265:             return Math.min(base, 40);
 266:         }
 267:         return base;
 268:     }

 276:             case LEARNING -> 50;
 277:             case UNAVAILABLE -> 40;
 278:         };
 279:         return Math.max(0, Math.min(100, base + result.confidenceAdjustment()));

  27:     /** FIX-109: Production parity is the default replay contract. Experimental mode is
  28:      * explicit so replay-only candidate rules can never contaminate a parity result. */
  29:     public enum ReplayLogicMode { PRODUCTION_PARITY, EXPERIMENTAL }

  33:     public Scope open(long runId, List<TradeSignal> replaySignals, Consumer<ExecutionOpportunity> opportunitySink) {
  34:         return open(runId, replaySignals, opportunitySink, ReplayLogicMode.PRODUCTION_PARITY);

  36: 
  37:     public Scope open(long runId, List<TradeSignal> replaySignals, Consumer<ExecutionOpportunity> opportunitySink,
  38:                       ReplayLogicMode logicMode) {
  39:         if (state.get() != null) throw new IllegalStateException("Execution replay scope is already active on this thread");
  40:         List<TradeSignal> immutable = replaySignals == null ? List.of() : replaySignals.stream()
  41:                 .filter(s -> s != null && s.getGeneratedAt() != null)
  42:                 .sorted(Comparator.comparing(TradeSignal::getGeneratedAt))
  43:                 .toList();
  44:         state.set(new State(runId, immutable, opportunitySink, logicMode == null ? ReplayLogicMode.PRODUCTION_PARITY : logicMode));
  45:         return new Scope();
  46:     }

  49:     public void recordStop(String symbol, long positionId, Instant at, String reason) {
  50:         if (!"STOP_LOSS".equals(reason)) return;
  51:         State s=required();
  52:         StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary previous=s.stops.get(symbol);
  53:         if (previous==null || !at.isBefore(previous.executedAt()))
  54:             s.stops.put(symbol,new StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary(positionId,at));
  55:     }
  56:     public StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary stopBoundary(String symbol, Instant asOf) {
  57:         StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary b=required().stops.get(symbol);
  58:         return b!=null && !b.executedAt().isAfter(asOf) ? b : null;
  59:     }

 107:         State s = required();

 109:         if (ref == null) return List.of();

 114:                 .limit(limit)
 115:                 .toList();

 155: 
 156:     /**
 157:      * FIX-043 production recovery path. Persists a missing historical signal while forcing
 158:      * every time-sensitive context lookup (sentiment, fundamentals, MTF/BTC context, etc.)
 159:      * to the recovered candle's original close/evaluation time.
 160:      *
 161:      * This is deliberately NOT equivalent to analyze(indicator), which uses Instant.now().
 162:      * Using current context to reconstruct a 12:21 candle at 12:30 would create a synthetic
 163:      * signal that Production could never have produced at 12:21 and would break Replay parity.
 164:      * The same buildSignal(...) implementation is shared; only temporal authority differs.
 165:      */
 166:     @Transactional
 167:     public TradeSignal analyzeRecovered(TechnicalIndicator indicator, Instant evaluationTime) {
 168:         if (indicator == null) {
 169:             throw new IllegalArgumentException("Recovered technical indicator is required");
 170:         }
 171:         IndicatorSnapshot snapshot = toSnapshot(indicator);
 172:         Instant asOf = evaluationTime == null ? snapshot.candleOpenTime() : evaluationTime;
 173:         TradeSignal signal = buildSignal(snapshot, asOf, true, false);
 174:         signal.setSignalMode("HISTORICAL_ONLY");
 175:         return signalRepository.save(signal);
 176:     }
 177: 
 178:     /**
 179:      * Regression-only analysis path. Runs the same production scoring/final-decision
 180:      * code but returns an unsaved TradeSignal so replay can never write to trade_signal.
 181:      */
 182:     @Transactional(readOnly = true)
 183:     public TradeSignal analyzeForRegression(IndicatorSnapshot snapshot, Instant evaluationTime) {
 184:         if (snapshot == null) {
 185:             throw new IllegalArgumentException("Regression indicator snapshot is required");
 186:         }
 187:         ReplayAnalysisProfiler profiler = replayAnalysisProfiler.get();
 188:         long startedNs = profiler == null ? 0L : System.nanoTime();
 189:         try {
 190:             return buildSignal(snapshot, evaluationTime == null ? snapshot.candleOpenTime() : evaluationTime, true, true);
 191:         } finally {
 192:             if (profiler != null) {
 193:                 profiler.recordCall(System.nanoTime() - startedNs);
 194:             }
 195:         }
 196:     }

 200:      * This activates timing observation; it does not alter buildSignal inputs, outputs,

 202:      */
 203:     public void beginReplayAnalysisProfiling(long runId) {
 204:         replayAnalysisProfiler.set(new ReplayAnalysisProfiler(runId));
 205:     }

===== src/main/java/com/crypto/service/RegimeStateService.java:1-100 =====
   1: package com.crypto.service;
   2: 
   3: import com.crypto.domain.MarketRegime;
   4: import com.crypto.dto.MarketRegimeAssessment;
   5: import com.crypto.execution.service.ExecutionReplayScope;
   6: import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value;
   7: import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
   8: 
   9: import java.time.Instant;
  10: import java.util.HashMap;
  11: import java.util.List;
  12: import java.util.Map;
  13: 
  14: /**
  15:  * FIX-091 / Fix 4 + FIX-109: Experimental-Replay-only regime persistence state machine.
  16:  * Production behavior is intentionally untouched until the parity gate is passed.
  17:  * State is keyed by replay run + symbol + interval and advances only on candle timestamps.
  18:  */
  19: @Service
  20: public class RegimeStateService {
  21:     private final ExecutionReplayScope replayScope;
  22:     private final int requiredCandles;
  23:     // Run id is part of the key so concurrent Replay/Test workers cannot reset or contaminate
  24:     // one another. The map contains only Replay state; Production never reads it.
  25:     private final Map<String, RegimeState> replayState = new HashMap<>();
  26: 
  27:     public RegimeStateService(ExecutionReplayScope replayScope,
  28:                               @Value("${analysis.regime.persistence-candles:3}") int requiredCandles) {
  29:         this.replayScope = replayScope;
  30:         this.requiredCandles = Math.max(2, requiredCandles);
  31:     }
  32: 
  33:     public synchronized Decision apply(String symbol, String interval, Instant candleTime,
  34:                                        MarketRegimeAssessment detectedAssessment) {
  35:         if (detectedAssessment == null || !replayScope.active() || !replayScope.experimental()) {
  36:             MarketRegime regime = detectedAssessment == null ? MarketRegime.UNKNOWN : detectedAssessment.regime();
  37:             return new Decision(detectedAssessment, regime, null, regime, 0, false);
  38:         }
  39: 
  40:         long runId = replayScope.runId();
  41:         String key = runId + "|" + symbol + "|" + interval;
  42:         RegimeState state = replayState.get(key);
  43:         MarketRegime detected = detectedAssessment.regime();
  44:         if (state == null) {
  45:             state = new RegimeState(detected, null, 0, candleTime);
  46:             replayState.put(key, state);
  47:             return new Decision(detectedAssessment, detected, null, detected, 0, false);
  48:         }
  49: 
  50:         MarketRegime confirmed = state.confirmed;
  51:         MarketRegime candidate = state.candidate;
  52:         int count = state.candidateCount;
  53:         boolean promoted = false;
  54: 
  55:         if (detected == confirmed) {
  56:             candidate = null;
  57:             count = 0;
  58:         } else if (detected == candidate) {
  59:             count++;
  60:         } else {
  61:             candidate = detected;
  62:             count = 1;
  63:         }
  64: 
  65:         if (candidate != null && count >= requiredCandles) {
  66:             confirmed = candidate;
  67:             candidate = null;
  68:             count = 0;
  69:             promoted = true;
  70:         }
  71:         replayState.put(key, new RegimeState(confirmed, candidate, count, candleTime));
  72: 
  73:         MarketRegimeAssessment effective = detected == confirmed
  74:                 ? detectedAssessment
  75:                 : new MarketRegimeAssessment(confirmed, detectedAssessment.confidence(),
  76:                     append(detectedAssessment.evidence(), "FIX-091 Replay persistence: detected=" + detected
  77:                             + ", candidate=" + candidate + ", confirmed=" + confirmed
  78:                             + ", candidateCount=" + count + "/" + requiredCandles + "."));
  79:         return new Decision(effective, detected, candidate, confirmed, count, promoted);
  80:     }
  81: 
  82:     private List<String> append(List<String> evidence, String value) {
  83:         java.util.ArrayList<String> result = new java.util.ArrayList<>(evidence == null ? List.of() : evidence);
  84:         result.add(value);
  85:         return List.copyOf(result);
  86:     }
  87: 
  88:     private record RegimeState(MarketRegime confirmed, MarketRegime candidate, int candidateCount, Instant lastCandleTime) {}
  89: 
  90:     public record Decision(MarketRegimeAssessment effectiveAssessment,
  91:                            MarketRegime detectedRegime,
  92:                            MarketRegime candidateRegime,
  93:                            MarketRegime confirmedRegime,
  94:                            int candidateCount,
  95:                            boolean promoted) {}
  96: }

  62:     public ValidationResult validateBuy(TradeSignal executionSignal, int entryQualityScore) {

  98:         }

===== src/main/java/com/crypto/execution/service/EntryConsumptionPolicy.java:1-60 =====
   1: package com.crypto.execution.service;
   2: 
   3: import com.crypto.execution.domain.EntryConsumptionState;
   4: import com.crypto.wallet.repository.WalletTradeRepository;
   5: import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
   6: import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
   7: 
   8: /**
   9:  * FIX-112A: consumption belongs to the specific bullish signal, not to the
  10:  * symbol's general position state. A pre-existing position can coexist with a
  11:  * blocked progressive-add signal, so OPEN-position state is not proof that the
  12:  * immediately previous bullish signal actually executed.
  13:  */
  14: @Service
  15: @RequiredArgsConstructor
  16: public class EntryConsumptionPolicy {
  17:     private final WalletTradeRepository walletTradeRepository;
  18:     private final ExecutionReplayScope replayScope;
  19: 
  20:     public EntryConsumptionState resolve(Long previousSignalId) {
  21:         if (previousSignalId == null) return EntryConsumptionState.NOT_CONSUMED;
  22:         if (replayScope.active()) {
  23:             return replayScope.entryConsumed(previousSignalId)
  24:                     ? EntryConsumptionState.CONSUMED
  25:                     : EntryConsumptionState.NOT_CONSUMED;
  26:         }
  27:         // Production authority is the immutable executed BUY row keyed to the

===== src/main/java/com/crypto/position/service/PositionPriceAuthorityPolicy.java:1-55 =====
   1: package com.crypto.position.service;
   2: 
   3: import com.crypto.domain.TradeSignal;
   4: import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
   5: 
   6: import java.time.Duration;
   7: import java.time.Instant;
   8: 
   9: /**
  10:  * Single authority for deciding whether a TradeSignal price is temporally valid for
  11:  * price-based position protection (TP / SL / profit-lock / replay mark-to-market).
  12:  *
  13:  * IMPORTANT INCIDENT GUARD (ALLOUSDT 2026-08-17): a 5m signal generated at 18:23:28
  14:  * carried the close of its 18:15-18:19 candle (0.2806). A position opened at 18:23:26
  15:  * at 0.2822 was incorrectly stopped four seconds later because that historical candle
  16:  * close was treated as a current market price. Signal prices remain valid as ANALYSIS
  17:  * CONTEXT, but a candle whose market observation time predates the position may never
  18:  * drive mechanical protection.
  19:  */
  20: @Service
  21: public class PositionPriceAuthorityPolicy {
  22: 
  23:     public boolean canUseSignalPrice(TradeSignal signal, Instant positionOpenedAt) {
  24:         if (signal == null || positionOpenedAt == null || signal.getLatestPrice() == null
  25:                 || signal.getLatestPrice().signum() <= 0) {
  26:             return false;
  27:         }
  28:         Instant observedAt = marketObservationTime(signal);
  29:         return observedAt != null && !observedAt.isBefore(positionOpenedAt);
  30:     }
  31: 
  32:     /** Candle close/observation time, derived from candle open time + interval. */
  33:     public Instant marketObservationTime(TradeSignal signal) {
  34:         if (signal == null || signal.getCandleOpenTime() == null) return null;
  35:         Duration interval = intervalDuration(signal.getInterval());
  36:         return interval == null ? null : signal.getCandleOpenTime().plus(interval);
  37:     }
  38: 
  39:     private Duration intervalDuration(String interval) {
  40:         if (interval == null) return null;
  41:         return switch (interval.trim().toLowerCase()) {
  42:             case "1m" -> Duration.ofMinutes(1);
  43:             case "5m" -> Duration.ofMinutes(5);
  44:             case "15m" -> Duration.ofMinutes(15);
  45:             case "1h" -> Duration.ofHours(1);
  46:             case "4h" -> Duration.ofHours(4);
  47:             case "1d" -> Duration.ofDays(1);
  48:             default -> null;
  49:         };
  50:     }
  51: }

## FINDINGS

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** The response correctly preserves the rejection: it explicitly says it neither amends nor approves RPR-0.4. The draft remains implementation-forbidden. [Response](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/agent-docs/RPR-0.4_REVIEW_RESPONSE.md:9) [Draft](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/agent-docs/FINAL_CHANGE_SPECIFICATION.md:3)

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** CR-01, CR-02, CR-08, and CR-09 are handled correctly in principle: granular registry, shared-class Production impact, a single-symbol alternative, and user-approved retirement decisions are necessary.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** The response materially underhandles pre-write qualification. Replay currently generates and persists `analysis_test_signal` and `trade_signal_test` before the price-evidence guard runs. Calling this merely a “pre-write guard” correction is insufficient. [Worker flow](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/regression/service/RegressionTestWorker.java:174) [writes](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/regression/service/RegressionTestWorker.java:636) [guard](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/regression/service/RegressionTestWorker.java:195)

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** CR-03 is incomplete. Exact-price Replay performs live-price protection, then only a validated bearish 1m SELL on the signal path; Production’s signal path also has TP, profit-lock, stop-loss, hard-floor, SELL suppression, and progressive-add logic. [Replay](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/regression/service/ShadowProductionReplayService.java:232) [Production](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/PaperTradingService.java:170)

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** The response’s parity tiers are the right start, but it does not resolve the central isolation conflict. Production execution decisions depend on account-derived state—executed BUY consumption, managed position allocation, daily statistics, and opportunity memory—while Replay scope substitutes empty replay-local state. Therefore strict prohibition of Production wallet/position reads permits analysis/decision parity only unless independently captured immutable account evidence is explicitly allowed. [Consumption](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/execution/service/EntryConsumptionPolicy.java:20) [wallet execution inputs](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/wallet/service/WalletAutoExecutionService.java:86) [replay opportunity substitution](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/execution/service/ExecutionReplayScope.java:147)

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** CR-05 correctly preserves `NON_CANONICAL` and `PROTECTION_NOT_APPLIED` as exclusions from the accepted protection stream, but does not define accepted-event coverage, close-message identity, or a certifiable tie rule. `find()` selects in `observed_at, id` order but returns only time, price, and source—discarding both `id` and `ordering_slot`. [Price evidence](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/market/service/MarketPriceEventService.java:118)

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** Correction to my earlier wording: I cannot substantiate that equal-time rows are inherently non-deterministic. The source establishes database ID ordering on retrieval. The material defect is instead that retained Replay evidence cannot identify the selected row or prove that ID order was the Production application order.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** The response omits the live/historical order-book inversion. Missing live observations block entry with `INSUFFICIENT_DATA_HOLD`; missing historical observations return `UNAVAILABLE`. Persisted snapshots are also intentionally lossy when their asynchronous persistence queue rejects or write fails. ## EVIDENCE

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** Production protection is invoked before close-event publication, but the stored price event has neither the kline closed flag nor candle identity. Replay therefore cannot prove which price row was the closing message without new durable lineage or an explicit ambiguity refusal. [Ingestion order](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/BinanceKlineService.java:101) [stored event fields](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/market/service/MarketPriceEventService.java:121)

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** Replay fills using the last accepted price at or before the generated-signal time, whereas Production resolves price at execution wall-clock time. The next contract must name the fill-clock evidence and refusal rule. [Replay fill](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/regression/service/ShadowProductionReplayService.java:305) [Production fill](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/PaperTradingService.java:340)

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** Processing outcomes affect whether business logic runs at all: expired work bypasses `business.apply`, while lock/transaction outcomes are durably recorded. Those outcomes need a declared Replay reproduction/refusal treatment. [Coordinator](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/execution/processing/SignalProcessingCoordinator.java:97)

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** `ReplayParityValidator` is signal-only and has no caller in `src/main/java`; it cannot be the execution/lifecycle oracle. [Validator](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/regression/service/ReplayParityValidator.java:15)

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** Existing deployment evidence records JAR SHA-256 and selected command-line overrides, but not commit identity, external YAML, or environment configuration. A historical version-dependent claim must refuse when that mapping cannot be reconstructed. [Deploy record](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/scripts/deploy/Deploy-Trader.ps1:123)

## OBJECTIONS

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** Reject the response as a sufficient requirements basis. It drops the pre-write contradiction, does not require a complete no-I/O/side-effect inventory, and leaves wallet isolation incompatible with execution/lifecycle claims.

- **AGENT INFERENCE:** The next single specification must obtain these user decisions before authorizing a design:
  1. Parity target: frozen working tree or evidenced deployed JAR/configuration per window.
  2. Isolation boundary: absolute prohibition on Production wallet/position reads, or named read-only oracle/evidence reads.
  3. Permitted claim tiers: analysis parity, execution-decision parity, lifecycle parity, or simulation.
  4. No-look-ahead treatment for recovered/historical signals and unavailable context.
  5. Single-symbol first versus fully specified multi-symbol account scheduling.
  6. Default mode and each Replay-only retirement.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** The next specification must include a master registry for:
  - preflight qualification before business-output writes, with a narrowly defined refusal audit record and a no-write regression test;
  - per-tier oracle records: source identity, as-of time, availability time, equality rule, missing-evidence result, and account-state eligibility;
  - immutable price-event IDs, ordering slots, source era, close-message linkage, accepted-stream coverage, tie/refusal rules, and separate signal-price mechanics;
  - full orchestration: protection, signal exits, price authority, fill/open-time clocks, expiry/retry/rollback outcomes, daily-budget creation timing, daily-loss failure, global caps, progressive adds, and stop-history boundary;
  - field-by-field context handling for derivatives, order book, sentiment, BTC, and higher timeframes: inject persisted evidence, recompute from sufficient as-of evidence, or refuse;
  - deny-by-default external I/O and side effects, including HTTP, mutable wallet settings, shared-table writes, and in-process metrics;
  - baseline/deployment/configuration/era rules, including V106–V109 and the FIX-154 source/document discrepancy;
  - Production impact, transaction/latency analysis, rollback, resource/cancellation safeguards, and disposable-MySQL validation;
  - explicit refusal/mismatch-rate reporting and documentation updates only after implementation becomes authorized.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** The response should remove the RegimeState reset item. It should distinguish ordinary daily-budget decline from the daily-loss path that throws; they are not the same failure mode. [Daily-loss call](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/PaperTradingService.java:322)

## UNKNOWNS

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** No database, production system, runtime, deployment record, build, test, or network resource was accessed in this review.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** The deployed JAR/configuration, actual Flyway history, evidence-era dates, and FIX-154 deployment status remain unverified.

- **AGENT INFERENCE:** With strict wallet/position isolation, lifecycle parity will usually be unavailable unless independently captured immutable starting-state and constraint evidence is approved and shown sufficient.
